# Math review round 14 at f9b30af

Four math-reviewer agents (workflow math-final, read-only, one per batch) back-translated the 2357 pending or stale
declarations at f9b30af0, listed by `node scripts/math-pending.mjs --batches 4` after the modules still in flux were
held back (see docs/team/handoffs/math-final.md). For each declaration the reviewer wrote the math from the Lean
signature and body first, then compared it with the docstring's $$ math clause by clause: binders and domains, every
hypothesis, ℕ subtraction and division, Option/none cases, list vs set and order, iff vs one direction, and data
stated in full rather than only by shape or length (the r12/r13 standard). Method as in rounds 3 to 13. Every
`stmtHash` and `mathHash` comes from the batch files, which match the `lake exe mathview` export e8d99a189953f827 at
f9b30af0. The four batch records were merged into this one note because `build-math-view.mjs` refuses two records
with the same round. The disagree rows were fixed in the docstrings by task math-r14-fix in the same commit as this
record, so those rows show as review stale until the next round.

## Disagreement kinds

- Batch A: most are definitions whose math elides a table or a case; three statements miss a hypothesis or state a
  weaker conclusion (align_partition_a, align_partition_b, align_self); three use integer subtraction where Lean
  truncates (Retry); one is a wrong case split (wkLayerStyle).
- Batch B: structures and examples with fields left out (Next.Branch, ex, pnAgent, exactRate, Reads.wf/ep/rd); data
  given only by shape or length (Intent.cases, WktrLayout.cells, expects, engineSpecLines); a missing recursion case
  (slowLatest); a missing hypothesis 0 < k (parallel_no_gain); a stronger claim than proved (respawn_coreBase);
  unstated reason strings (chooseBase); shortened strings (Incidents.vocab).
- Batch C: data stated only by length or a placeholder (registry, engineFiles, crStages, wkStages, refutedPremises,
  PeopleCard.goldenLines, the eight Signals timelines, parseDataset); a list order that differs (engineCites); fields
  left out (four Premise golden rows omit `name`, RoleRow); a mapping given only as a range (Stage.name, Kind.name,
  State.name) or with unstated cases (Reason.subject, parseSource); a set where the Lean is a filtered list
  (Reference.partsWith, wrong for c = agree); a fold override order not stated (expandGroups); a substitution that
  reads as the identity (classify_metaFinished).
- Batch D: data tables given only as a shape or a length (Trigger.table, Trigger.cases, PeopleDeck.goldenLines); a
  set where the order is written into the generated workflows (ciMain); missing cases (sysPiece, setMap,
  sampleCone). `aiWords` and `fillers` are written as sets but only used for membership tests, so they are agreed.
